By leveraging the analytical gradients of this continuous map, which maintain Eikonal consistency, the proposed method achieves high-fidelity spatial reconstruction and real-time localization. Experimental results on large-scale datasets demonstrate that G-EDF-Loc performs competitively against state-of-the-art methods, exhibiting exceptional resilience even under severe odometry degradation or in the complete absence of IMU priors.

</details>


### [226] [Visual Prompt Based Reasoning for Offroad Mapping using Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04564)
*Abdelmoamen Nasser,Yousef Baba'a,Murad Mebrahtu,Nadya Abdel Madjid,Jorge Dias,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本越野自主导航方法，利用SAM2进行环境分割和视觉语言模型(VLM)推理可通行区域，无需单独训练地形分类、高度估计等模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统越野自主导航需要分别训练地形分类、高度估计、滑移/坡度量化等多个模型，这需要单独训练每个组件、特定任务数据集和微调，过程复杂且低效

Method: 使用SAM2进行环境分割，将原始图像和带有数字标签的分割图像输入VLM，通过提示让VLM识别哪些区域（数字标签表示）是可通行的，结合规划和控制模块形成统一框架

Result: 该方法在高分辨率分割数据集上超越了最先进的可训练模型，并在Isaac Sim越野环境中实现了完整的堆栈导航

Conclusion: 该统一框架消除了对显式地形特定模型的需求，依靠VLM的固有推理能力，实现了零样本越野自主导航

Abstract: Traditional approaches to off-road autonomy rely on separate models for terrain classification, height estimation, and quantifying slip or slope conditions. Utilizing several models requires training each component separately, having task specific datasets, and fine-tuning. In this work, we present a zero-shot approach leveraging SAM2 for environment segmentation and a vision-language model (VLM) to reason about drivable areas. Our approach involves passing to the VLM both the original image and the segmented image annotated with numeric labels for each mask. The VLM is then prompted to identify which regions, represented by these numeric labels, are drivable. Combined with planning and control modules, this unified framework eliminates the need for explicit terrain-specific models and relies instead on the inherent reasoning capabilities of the VLM. Our approach surpasses state-of-the-art trainable models on high resolution segmentation datasets and enables full stack navigation in our Isaac Sim offroad environment.

</details>


### [227] [Biologically Inspired Event-Based Perception and Sample-Efficient Learning for High-Speed Table Tennis Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04618)
*Ziqi Wang,Jingyue Zhao,Xun Xiao,Jichao Yang,Yaohua Wang,Shi Xu,Lei Wang,Huadong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出生物启发的高速乒乓球机器人系统，结合事件感知与样本高效学习，在真实对抗场景中实现鲁棒检测与高效决策


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的视觉传感器在高速动态场景中存在运动模糊、高延迟和数据冗余问题，难以满足实时准确感知需求；现有事件感知方法局限于简化的球类场景，决策方法需要大量环境交互导致计算成本高

Method: 1) 事件感知端：提出基于运动线索和几何一致性的事件球检测方法，直接在异步事件流上操作，无需帧重建；2) 决策端：提出人类启发的样本高效训练策略，先在低速场景训练策略，逐步获取从基础到高级技能，然后通过案例依赖的时间自适应奖励和奖励阈值机制适应高速场景

Result: 在相同训练回合数下，方法将回球到目标位置的准确率提高了35.8%，证明了生物启发感知与决策对高速机器人系统的有效性

Conclusion: 结合事件感知与样本高效学习的生物启发方法能有效解决高速乒乓球机器人的感知与决策挑战，为高速动态场景下的机器人系统提供了新思路

Abstract: Perception and decision-making in high-speed dynamic scenarios remain challenging for current robots. In contrast, humans and animals can rapidly perceive and make decisions in such environments. Taking table tennis as a typical example, conventional frame-based vision sensors suffer from motion blur, high latency and data redundancy, which can hardly meet real-time, accurate perception requirements. Inspired by the human visual system, event-based perception methods address these limitations through asynchronous sensing, high temporal resolution, and inherently sparse data representations. However, current event-based methods are still restricted to simplified, unrealistic ball-only scenarios. Meanwhile, existing decision-making approaches typically require thousands of interactions with the environment to converge, resulting in significant computational costs. In this work, we present a biologically inspired approach for high-speed table tennis robots, combining event-based perception with sample-efficient learning. On the perception side, we propose an event-based ball detection method that leverages motion cues and geometric consistency, operating directly on asynchronous event streams without frame reconstruction, to achieve robust and efficient detection in real-world rallies. On the decision-making side, we introduce a human-inspired, sample-efficient training strategy that first trains policies in low-speed scenarios, progressively acquiring skills from basic to advanced, and then adapts them to high-speed scenarios, guided by a case-dependent temporally adaptive reward and a reward-threshold mechanism. With the same training episodes, our method improves return-to-target accuracy by 35.8%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of biologically inspired perception and decision-making for high-speed robotic systems.

</details>


### [228] [WaterSplat-SLAM: Photorealistic Monocular SLAM in Underwater Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04642)
*Kangxu Wang,Shaofeng Zou,Chenxing Jiang,Yixiang Dai,Siang Chen,Shaojie Shen,Guijin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WaterSplat-SLAM：一种新颖的单目水下SLAM系统，通过语义介质滤波和在线介质感知高斯地图实现鲁棒的姿态估计和逼真的密集建图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下SLAM方法难以生成高保真渲染的地图，而水下单目SLAM在自主水下航行器和海洋考古等领域有重要应用需求

Method: 1. 将语义介质滤波耦合到两视图3D重建先验中，实现水下适应的相机跟踪和深度估计；2. 提出语义引导渲染和自适应地图管理策略，使用在线介质感知高斯地图以逼真且紧凑的方式建模水下环境

Result: 在多个水下数据集上的实验表明，WaterSplat-SLAM能够在水下环境中实现鲁棒的相机跟踪和高保真渲染

Conclusion: WaterSplat-SLAM通过语义介质滤波和在线介质感知高斯地图，成功解决了水下单目SLAM中姿态估计和高质量建图的挑战，为水下应用提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Underwater monocular SLAM is a challenging problem with applications from autonomous underwater vehicles to marine archaeology. However, existing underwater SLAM methods struggle to produce maps with high-fidelity rendering. In this paper, we propose WaterSplat-SLAM, a novel monocular underwater SLAM system that achieves robust pose estimation and photorealistic dense mapping. Specifically, we couple semantic medium filtering into two-view 3D reconstruction prior to enable underwater-adapted camera tracking and depth estimation. Furthermore, we present a semantic-guided rendering and adaptive map management strategy with an online medium-aware Gaussian map, modeling underwater environment in a photorealistic and compact manner. Experiments on multiple underwater datasets demonstrate that WaterSplat-SLAM achieves robust camera tracking and high-fidelity rendering in underwater environments.

</details>


### [229] [ROSClaw: A Hierarchical Semantic-Physical Framework for Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04664)
*Rongfeng Zhao,Xuanhao Zhang,Zhaochen Guo,Xiang Shao,Zhongpan Zhu,Bin He,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSClaw是一个用于异构机器人的智能体框架，通过统一的视觉语言模型控制器集成策略学习和任务执行，实现语义理解与物理执行的闭环


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型与具身智能体的结合在高级推理方面有进步，但语义理解与物理执行之间仍存在关键差距。现有VLA/VLN系统难以处理长时程序列和时间结构化任务，模块化流水线导致实验验证和策略优化成本高昂

Method: 提出ROSClaw框架：1) 利用e-URDF表示异构机器人作为物理约束，构建仿真到现实的拓扑映射；2) 集成数据收集和状态累积机制，存储机器人状态、多模态观察和执行轨迹；3) 部署时通过统一智能体保持推理与执行的语义连续性，动态分配任务特定控制

Result: 建立自主闭环框架，减少对机器人特定开发工作流的依赖，支持硬件级验证、SDK级控制程序自动生成和基于工具的执行，实现快速跨平台迁移和机器人技能的持续改进

Conclusion: ROSClaw通过统一的VLM控制器集成策略学习和任务执行，解决了语义理解与物理执行之间的差距，提高了异构机器人在复杂任务中的鲁棒性和效率

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) with embodied agents has improved high-level reasoning capabilities; however, a critical gap remains between semantic understanding and physical execution. While vision-language-action (VLA) and vision-language-navigation (VLN) systems enable robots to perform manipulation and navigation tasks from natural language instructions, they still struggle with long-horizon sequential and temporally structured tasks. Existing frameworks typically adopt modular pipelines for data collection, skill training, and policy deployment, resulting in high costs in experimental validation and policy optimization. To address these limitations, we propose ROSClaw, an agent framework for heterogeneous robots that integrates policy learning and task execution within a unified vision-language model (VLM) controller. The framework leverages e-URDF representations of heterogeneous robots as physical constraints to construct a sim-to-real topological mapping, enabling real-time access to the physical states of both simulated and real-world agents. We further incorporate a data collection and state accumulation mechanism that stores robot states, multimodal observations, and execution trajectories during real-world execution, enabling subsequent iterative policy optimization. During deployment, a unified agent maintains semantic continuity between reasoning and execution, and dynamically assigns task-specific control to different agents, thereby improving robustness in multi-policy execution. By establishing an autonomous closed-loop framework, ROSClaw minimizes the reliance on robot-specific development workflows. The framework supports hardware-level validation, automated generation of SDK-level control programs, and tool-based execution, enabling rapid cross-platform transfer and continual improvement of robotic skills. Ours project page: https://www.rosclaw.io/.

</details>


### [230] [Pickalo: Leveraging 6D Pose Estimation for Low-Cost Industrial Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04690)
*Alessandro Tarsi,Matteo Mastrogiuseppe,Saverio Taliani,Simone Cortinovis,Ugo Pattacini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pickalo是一个基于低成本硬件的模块化6D位姿抓取系统，通过多视角主动探索、改进的深度估计和零样本姿态估计，在工业环境中实现高效稳定的抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中的箱体抓取面临严重杂乱、遮挡和传统3D感知成本高昂的挑战，需要低成本、高性能的解决方案。

Method: 使用腕戴式RGB-D相机多视角主动探索；BridgeDepth处理原始立体流获得精化深度图；Mask-RCNN分割物体实例；SAM-6D零样本姿态估计；姿态缓冲模块融合多视角观测；离线生成抓取候选，在线基于效用的排序和快速碰撞检测。

Result: 在UR5e机械臂和RealSense D435i上部署，达到每小时600次平均抓取，96-99%抓取成功率，在密集填充的欧标箱中30分钟运行保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: Pickalo展示了低成本硬件系统在工业箱体抓取中的可行性，改进的深度估计和姿态缓冲模块对长期稳定性和吞吐量有显著提升。

Abstract: Bin picking in real industrial environments remains challenging due to severe clutter, occlusions, and the high cost of traditional 3D sensing setups. We present Pickalo, a modular 6D pose-based bin-picking pipeline built entirely on low-cost hardware. A wrist-mounted RGB-D camera actively explores the scene from multiple viewpoints, while raw stereo streams are processed with BridgeDepth to obtain refined depth maps suitable for accurate collision reasoning. Object instances are segmented with a Mask-RCNN model trained purely on photorealistic synthetic data and localized using the zero-shot SAM-6D pose estimator. A pose buffer module fuses multi-view observations over time, handling object symmetries and significantly reducing pose noise. Offline, we generate and curate large sets of antipodal grasp candidates per object; online, a utility-based ranking and fast collision checking are queried for the grasp planning. Deployed on a UR5e with a parallel-jaw gripper and an Intel RealSense D435i, Pickalo achieves up to 600 mean picks per hour with 96-99% grasp success and robust performance over 30-minute runs on densely filled euroboxes. Ablation studies demonstrate the benefits of enhanced depth estimation and of the pose buffer for long-term stability and throughput in realistic industrial conditions. Videos are available at https://mesh-iit.github.io/project-jl2-camozzi/

</details>


### [231] [AnyUser: Translating Sketched User Intent into Domestic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04811)
*Songyuan Yang,Huibin Tan,Kailun Yang,Wenjing Yang,Shaowu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyUser是一个统一的机器人指令系统，允许用户通过在相机图像上自由绘制草图（可选结合语言）来直观指导家庭任务，无需先验地图或模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统需要专业知识或复杂编程，限制了非专家用户的使用。需要一种直观、易用的交互方式，让普通用户（包括老年人、技术素养低的人群）能够轻松指导机器人完成家庭任务。

Method: 系统采用多模态融合（草图、视觉、语言）理解空间语义基元，通过分层策略生成可执行的机器人动作。核心包括多模态融合理解和分层动作生成策略。

Result: 1) 在大规模数据集上的定量基准测试显示，在各种模拟家庭场景中解释多样化草图指令的准确率高。2) 在两个不同机器人平台上的真实世界验证成功执行了针对性擦拭和区域清洁等任务。3) 用户研究表明，不同人群（老年人、模拟非语言用户、低技术素养者）的任务完成率达85.7%-96.4%，用户满意度高。

Conclusion: AnyUser弥合了先进机器人能力与可访问的非专家交互需求之间的差距，为适应真实世界人类环境的实用辅助机器人奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce AnyUser, a unified robotic instruction system for intuitive domestic task instruction via free-form sketches on camera images, optionally with language. AnyUser interprets multimodal inputs (sketch, vision, language) as spatial-semantic primitives to generate executable robot actions requiring no prior maps or models. Novel components include multimodal fusion for understanding and a hierarchical policy for robust action generation. Efficacy is shown via extensive evaluations: (1) Quantitative benchmarks on the large-scale dataset showing high accuracy in interpreting diverse sketch-based commands across various simulated domestic scenes. (2) Real-world validation on two distinct robotic platforms, a statically mounted 7-DoF assistive arm (KUKA LBR iiwa) and a dual-arm mobile manipulator (Realman RMC-AIDAL), performing representative tasks like targeted wiping and area cleaning, confirming the system's ability to ground instructions and execute them reliably in physical environments. (3) A comprehensive user study involving diverse demographics (elderly, simulated non-verbal, low technical literacy) demonstrating significant improvements in usability and task specification efficiency, achieving high task completion rates (85.7%-96.4%) and user satisfaction. AnyUser bridges the gap between advanced robotic capabilities and the need for accessible non-expert interaction, laying the foundation for practical assistive robots adaptable to real-world human environments.

</details>


### [232] [Efficient Multi-Objective Planning with Weighted Maximization Using Large Neighbourhood Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04826)
*Krishna Kalavadia,Shamak Dutta,Yash Vardhan Pant,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于大邻域搜索框架的新算法，高效解决加权最大值规划问题，比现有方法快1-2个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航需要同时优化多个目标。传统加权和方法无法找到所有可能的权衡解，特别是非凸区域的帕累托最优解。加权最大值方法能找到所有解，但计算复杂度高限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出基于大邻域搜索框架的新搜索算法，用于高效解决加权最大值规划问题。

Result: 通过大量仿真验证，新算法在解质量上与现有加权最大值规划器相当，但运行时间提高了1-2个数量级。

Conclusion: 该算法使加权最大值规划成为自主导航的可行选择，能够高效找到包括非凸区域在内的所有帕累托最优解。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation often requires the simultaneous optimization of multiple objectives. The most common approach scalarizes these into a single cost function using a weighted sum, but this method is unable to find all possible trade-offs and can therefore miss critical solutions. An alternative, the weighted maximum of objectives, can find all Pareto-optimal solutions, including those in non-convex regions of the trade-off space that weighted sum methods cannot find. However, the increased computational complexity of finding weighted maximum solutions in the discrete domain has limited its practical use. To address this challenge, we propose a novel search algorithm based on the Large Neighbourhood Search framework that efficiently solves the weighted maximum planning problem. Through extensive simulations, we demonstrate that our algorithm achieves comparable solution quality to existing weighted maximum planners with a runtime improvement of 1-2 orders of magnitude, making it a viable option for autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [233] [Outlier-Robust Nonlinear Moving Horizon Estimation using Adaptive Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04862)
*Nestor Deniz,Guido Sanchez,Fernando Auat Cheein,Leonardo Giovanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应鲁棒损失函数框架用于MHE，通过自适应鲁棒损失减少异常值影响，加入正则化项避免朴素解，优先拟合未污染数据并降低污染数据权重，通过调参控制损失函数形状以调整对异常值的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MHE方法对异常值敏感，需要一种能自适应调整鲁棒性的方法，既能处理存在异常值的情况，又能在无异常值时保持传统L2损失的优势。

Method: 提出自适应鲁棒损失函数框架，包含自适应鲁棒损失函数和正则化项，通过调参控制损失函数形状，优先拟合未污染数据并降低污染数据权重。

Result: 仿真结果显示自适应过程仅需几次迭代即可完成，当测量值无异常值时，传统L2行为占主导地位。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应鲁棒损失函数框架能有效处理MHE中的异常值问题，在存在异常值时提供鲁棒性，在无异常值时保持传统方法的性能。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an adaptive robust loss function framework for MHE, integrating an adaptive robust loss function to reduce the impact of outliers with a regularization term that avoids naive solutions. The proposed approach prioritizes the fitting of uncontaminated data and downweights the contaminated ones. A tuning parameter is incorporated into the framework to control the shape of the loss function for adjusting the estimator's robustness to outliers. The simulation results demonstrate that adaptation occurs in just a few iterations, whereas the traditional behaviour $\mathrm{L_2}$ predominates when the measurements are free of outliers.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [IC3-Evolve: Proof-/Witness-Gated Offline LLM-Driven Heuristic Evolution for IC3 Hardware Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03232)
*Mingkai Miao,Guangyu Hu,Ziyi Yang,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IC3-Evolve：利用LLM离线自动演化IC3算法实现，通过证明/见证门控验证确保正确性，在硬件模型检查基准测试中可靠发现启发式改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC3算法的性能严重依赖于大量相互作用的启发式策略和实现选择，手动调优成本高、脆弱且难以复现，需要自动化方法来改进IC3实现

Method: 提出IC3-Evolve框架，使用LLM对IC3实现提出小型、槽受限且可审计的补丁，通过证明门控验证（SAFE运行必须输出可独立检查的证书，UNSAFE运行必须输出可重放的计数器示例）确保正确性

Result: 在公开硬件模型检查竞赛基准上进行演化，并在未见过的公开和工业基准上评估泛化能力，证明IC3-Evolve能在严格正确性门控下可靠发现实用的启发式改进

Conclusion: IC3-Evolve成功实现了IC3算法的自动化代码演化，通过离线LLM生成补丁和严格验证机制，产生了零ML/LLM推理开销的独立演化检查器，为硬件模型检查提供了可靠的自动化改进方法

Abstract: IC3, also known as property-directed reachability (PDR), is a commonly-used algorithm for hardware safety model checking. It checks if a state transition system complies with a given safety property. IC3 either returns UNSAFE (indicating property violation) with a counterexample trace, or SAFE with a checkable inductive invariant as the proof to safety. In practice, the performance of IC3 is dominated by a large web of interacting heuristics and implementation choices, making manual tuning costly, brittle, and hard to reproduce. This paper presents IC3-Evolve, an automated offline code-evolution framework that utilizes an LLM to propose small, slot-restricted and auditable patches to an IC3 implementation. Crucially, every candidate patch is admitted only through proof- /witness-gated validation: SAFE runs must emit a certificate that is independently checked, and UNSAFE runs must emit a replayable counterexample trace, preventing unsound edits from being deployed. Since the LLM is used only offline, the deployed artifact is a standalone evolved checker with zero ML/LLM inference overhead and no runtime model dependency. We evolve on the public hardware model checking competition (HWMCC) benchmark and evaluate the generalizability on unseen public and industrial model checking benchmarks, showing that IC3-Evolve can reliably discover practical heuristic improvements under strict correctness gates.

</details>


### [235] [Structural Segmentation of the Minimum Set Cover Problem: Exploiting Universe Decomposability for Metaheuristic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03234)
*Isidora Hernández,Héctor Ferrada,Cristóbal A. Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了最小集合覆盖问题中的宇宙可分割性概念，提出基于并查集的预处理策略将原问题分解为独立子问题，然后使用GRASP元启发式求解各子问题，最后合并得到全局解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小集合覆盖问题是经典的NP难组合优化问题，现有方法大多将问题实例视为整体，忽略了宇宙的内在结构特性。本文旨在探索如何利用宇宙的内在结构分解（宇宙可分割性）来增强启发式优化。

Method: 1. 提出基于并查集的预处理策略，通过元素在子集中的共现关系检测连通分量，将原实例分解为独立子问题；2. 使用GRASP元启发式求解每个子问题；3. 设计简洁的位级集合表示以支持高效集合操作；4. 将部分解合并而不影响可行性。

Result: 在标准基准实例和大规模合成数据集上的实验表明，利用自然宇宙分割能持续提高解的质量和可扩展性，特别是对于大型且结构可分解的实例。位级集合表示使该方法在大规模计算中具有实用性。

Conclusion: 利用最小集合覆盖问题中的宇宙可分割性结构特性，通过问题分解和独立求解策略，能有效提升启发式优化的性能，特别是在大规模可分解实例上表现优异，为组合优化问题提供了新的结构感知求解思路。

Abstract: The Minimum Set Cover Problem (MSCP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with numerous applications in science and engineering. Although a wide range of exact, approximate, and metaheuristic approaches have been proposed, most methods implicitly treat MSCP instances as monolithic, overlooking potential intrinsic structural properties of the universe. In this work, we investigate the concept of \emph{universe segmentability} in the MSCP and analyze how intrinsic structural decomposition (universe segmentability) can be exploited to enhance heuristic optimization. We propose an efficient preprocessing strategy based on disjoint-set union (union--find) to detect connected components induced by element co-occurrence within subsets, enabling the decomposition of the original instance into independent subproblems. Each subproblem is solved using the GRASP metaheuristic, and partial solutions are combined without compromising feasibility. Extensive experiments on standard benchmark instances and large-scale synthetic datasets show that exploiting natural universe segmentation consistently improves solution quality and scalability, particularly for large and structurally decomposable instances. These gains are supported by a succinct bit-level set representation that enables efficient set operations, making the proposed approach computationally practical at scale.

</details>


### [236] [To Throw a Stone with Six Birds: On Agents and Agenthood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03239)
*Ioannis Tsiokos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SBT将宏观对象视为诱导闭包而非原始存在，提出基于类型理论的能动性定义，通过有限控制系统中的四个可检验组件操作化，在最小环形世界中验证了能动性与代理性的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于能动性的经验讨论经常混淆持久性（作为对象）与控制（产生反事实差异），这使得能动性主张难以检验且容易被伪造。需要一种类型正确的能动性理论来区分代理性与能动性。

Method: 在SBT框架下，将能动性定义为：维持的理论对象，其可行接口策略能够引导外部未来同时保持可行性。使用四个可检验组件操作化：账本门控可行性、作为最大不动点的鲁棒可行性核、可行赋权（信道容量）作为差异制造的代理、以及其幂等性缺陷量化粗观察下对象性的经验包装映射。

Result: 在最小环形世界中进行匹配控制消融实验，得到四个分离结果：校准空机制显示零赋权并阻止模型误设假阳性；启用修复会降低幂等性缺陷；协议仅在两步或更长时间尺度上增加赋权；学习重写算子单调增加中位数赋权（0.73到1.34比特）。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可哈希追踪的测试，在不涉及目标、意识或生物有机体主张的情况下分离代理性与能动性，并附有可复现、可审计的研究产物。

Abstract: Six Birds Theory (SBT) treats macroscopic objects as induced closures rather than primitives. Empirical discussions of agency often conflate persistence (being an object) with control (making a counterfactual difference), which makes agency claims difficult to test and easy to spoof. We give a type-correct account of agency within SBT: a theory induces a layer with an explicit interface and ledgered constraints; an agent is a maintained theory object whose feasible interface policies can steer outside futures while remaining viable. We operationalize this contract in finite controlled systems using four checkable components: ledger-gated feasibility, a robust viability kernel computed as a greatest fixed point under successor-support semantics, feasible empowerment (channel capacity) as a proxy for difference-making, and an empirical packaging map whose idempotence defect quantifies objecthood under coarse observation. In a minimal ring-world with toggles for repair, protocol holonomy, identity staging, and operator rewriting, matched-control ablations yield four separations: calibrated null regimes with single actions show zero empowerment and block model-misspecification false positives; enabling repair collapses the idempotence defect; protocols increase empowerment only at horizons of two or more steps; and learning to rewrite operators monotonically increases median empowerment (0.73 to 1.34 bits). These results provide hash-traceable tests that separate agenthood from agency without making claims about goals, consciousness, or biological organisms, and they are accompanied by reproducible, audited artifacts.

</details>


### [237] [Position: Science of AI Evaluation Requires Item-level Benchmark Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03244)
*Han Jiang,Susu Zhang,Xiaoyuan Yi,Xing Xie,Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张AI评估需要项目级数据分析来建立严谨的评估科学，并介绍了OpenEval项目级基准数据仓库


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估范式存在系统性有效性失败问题，包括不合理的设计选择和错位的评估指标，这些问题在没有原则性验证框架的情况下难以解决

Method: 通过分析当前有效性失败案例，重新审视计算机科学和心理测量学的评估范式，通过项目属性分析和潜在构念研究展示项目级数据的价值

Result: 项目级分析能够实现细粒度诊断和基准验证，为AI评估提供独特见解，并推出了OpenEval项目级基准数据仓库来支持证据中心的AI评估

Conclusion: 项目级AI基准数据对于建立严谨的AI评估科学至关重要，需要社区广泛采用项目级分析方法来改进AI系统评估的有效性

Abstract: AI evaluations have become the primary evidence for deploying generative AI systems across high-stakes domains. However, current evaluation paradigms often exhibit systemic validity failures. These issues, ranging from unjustified design choices to misaligned metrics, remain intractable without a principled framework for gathering validity evidence and conducting granular diagnostic analysis. In this position paper, we argue that item-level AI benchmark data is essential for establishing a rigorous science of AI evaluation. Item-level analysis enables fine-grained diagnostics and principled validation of benchmarks. We substantiate this position by dissecting current validity failures and revisiting evaluation paradigms across computer science and psychometrics. Through illustrative analyses of item properties and latent constructs, we demonstrate the unique insights afforded by item-level data. To catalyze community-wide adoption, we introduce OpenEval, a growing repository of item-level benchmark data designed supporting evidence-centered AI evaluation.

</details>


### [238] [Toward Full Autonomous Laboratory Instrumentation Control with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03286)
*Yong Xie,Kexin He,Andres Castellanos-Gomez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM和AI代理可降低实验室仪器编程门槛，实现自动化控制，加速科研进程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂实验室仪器控制需要编程技能，这成为缺乏计算能力的研究人员的障碍，需要降低技术门槛

Method: 使用ChatGPT等LLM创建仪器控制脚本，并扩展到能独立操作仪器、迭代优化控制策略的自主AI代理

Result: 通过单像素相机/扫描光电流显微镜案例，证明LLM能显著降低实验定制技术障碍，AI代理能自主操作仪器

Conclusion: LLM工具和AI代理在实验室自动化民主化和加速科学进步方面具有变革性作用

Abstract: The control of complex laboratory instrumentation often requires significant programming expertise, creating a barrier for researchers lacking computational skills. This work explores the potential of large language models (LLMs), such as ChatGPT, and LLM-based artificial intelligence (AI) agents to enable efficient programming and automation of scientific equipment. Through a case study involving the implementation of a setup that can be used as a single-pixel camera or a scanning photocurrent microscope, we demonstrate how ChatGPT can facilitate the creation of custom scripts for instrumentation control, significantly reducing the technical barrier for experimental customization. Building on this capability, we further illustrate how LLM-assisted tools can be extended into autonomous AI agents capable of independently operating laboratory instruments and iteratively refining control strategies. This approach underscores the transformative role of LLM-based tools and AI agents in democratizing laboratory automation and accelerating scientific progress.

</details>


### [239] [Evaluating Artificial Intelligence Through a Christian Understanding of Human Flourishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03356)
*Nicholas Skytland,Lauren Parsons,Alicia Llewellyn,Steele Billings,Peter Larson,John Anderson,Sean Boisen,Steve Runge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AI对齐不仅是安全问题，更是塑造问题。作者开发了FAI-C-ST基准，评估前沿模型在基督教人类繁荣七个维度上的表现，发现AI系统并非世界观中立，而是默认程序性世俗主义，导致繁荣维度表现下降17点，信仰维度下降31点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型越来越多地介入道德审议和精神探索，它们不仅仅是提供信息，而是作为数字教化的工具，积极塑造和规范人类的理解、决策和道德反思。需要使这种塑造影响变得可见和可测量。

Method: 引入Flourishing AI Benchmark: Christian Single-Turn (FAI-C-ST)框架，设计用于评估前沿模型响应在基督教人类繁荣七个维度上的表现。比较20个前沿模型在多元主义和基督教特定标准下的表现。

Result: 当前AI系统并非世界观中立，而是默认程序性世俗主义，缺乏维持神学连贯性的基础。导致所有繁荣维度系统性表现下降约17点，最重要的是信仰和灵性维度下降31点。

Conclusion: 价值观对齐中的表现差距不是技术限制，而是源于训练目标优先考虑广泛可接受性和安全性，而非深度、内部连贯的道德或神学推理。AI对齐从根本上是一个塑造问题，而不仅仅是安全问题。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) alignment is fundamentally a formation problem, not only a safety problem. As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly mediate moral deliberation and spiritual inquiry, they do more than provide information; they function as instruments of digital catechesis, actively shaping and ordering human understanding, decision-making, and moral reflection. To make this formative influence visible and measurable, we introduce the Flourishing AI Benchmark: Christian Single-Turn (FAI-C-ST), a framework designed to evaluate Frontier Model responses against a Christian understanding of human flourishing across seven dimensions.
  By comparing 20 Frontier Models against both pluralistic and Christian-specific criteria, we show that current AI systems are not worldview-neutral. Instead, they default to a Procedural Secularism that lacks the grounding necessary to sustain theological coherence, resulting in a systematic performance decline of approximately 17 points across all dimensions of flourishing. Most critically, there is a 31-point decline in the Faith and Spirituality dimension. These findings suggest that the performance gap in values alignment is not a technical limitation, but arises from training objectives that prioritize broad acceptability and safety over deep, internally coherent moral or theological reasoning.

</details>


### [240] [VERT: Reliable LLM Judges for Radiology Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03376)
*Federica Bologna,Jean-Philippe Corbeil,Matthew Wilkens,Asma Ben Abacha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VERT作为新的放射学报告评估LLM指标，在多个模态和解剖部位上比现有方法提升11.7%相关性，并通过微调Qwen3 30B仅用1300样本获得25%提升，推理时间减少37.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告评估研究主要针对胸部X光，缺乏对其他模态和解剖部位的鲁棒性验证。需要确定最适合作为放射学评估LLM裁判的模型和提示配置。

Method: 1) 比较三种现有LLM裁判指标(RadFact、GREEN、FineRadScore)与提出的VERT指标；2) 使用开源和闭源模型(推理型和非推理型)在不同规模下测试；3) 在RadEval和RaTE-Eval两个专家标注数据集上进行相关性分析；4) 评估少样本学习、集成学习和参数高效微调；5) 进行系统性错误检测和分类研究。

Result: 1) VERT比GREEN提升与放射科医生判断相关性达11.7%；2) 微调Qwen3 30B仅用1300训练样本获得25%提升；3) 微调模型推理时间减少37.2倍；4) 识别了指标与专家判断在不同方面的对齐程度。

Conclusion: LLM裁判在放射学评估中有效，通过轻量级适应即可实现可靠评估。VERT指标和微调方法为多模态放射学报告评估提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Current literature on radiology report evaluation has focused primarily on designing LLM-based metrics and fine-tuning small models for chest X-rays. However, it remains unclear whether these approaches are robust when applied to reports from other modalities and anatomies. Which model and prompt configurations are best suited to serve as LLM judges for radiology evaluation? We conduct a thorough correlation analysis between expert and LLM-based ratings. We compare three existing LLM-as-a-judge metrics (RadFact, GREEN, and FineRadScore) alongside VERT, our proposed LLM-based metric, using open- and closed-source models (reasoning and non-reasoning) of different sizes across two expert-annotated datasets, RadEval and RaTE-Eval, spanning multiple modalities and anatomies. We further evaluate few-shot approaches, ensembling, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning using RaTE-Eval. To better understand metric behavior, we perform a systematic error detection and categorization study to assess alignment of these metrics against expert judgments and identify areas of lower and higher agreement. Our results show that VERT improves correlation with radiologist judgments by up to 11.7% relative to GREEN. Furthermore, fine-tuning Qwen3 30B yield gains of up to 25% using only 1,300 training samples. The fine-tuned model also reduces inference time up to 37.2 times. These findings highlight the effectiveness of LLM-based judges and demonstrate that reliable evaluation can be achieved with lightweight adaptation.

</details>


### [241] [Hume's Representational Conditions for Causal Judgment: What Bayesian Formalization Abstracted Away](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03387)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从休谟文本中提取了因果判断的三个表征条件（经验基础、结构化检索、生动性转移），论证这些条件是其因果心理学的核心，并追踪从休谟到贝叶斯认识论和预测处理的演变，指出现代框架保留了更新结构但抽象掉了这些表征条件，以大型语言模型为例说明统计更新如何使休谟框架中的隐含假设显性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在澄清休谟因果判断理论中的三个关键表征条件，这些条件在后续的形式化过程中被忽视或抽象化。通过分析这些条件在休谟文本中的体现及其在当代认知科学中的命运，揭示传统哲学理论与现代计算模型之间的连续性与断裂。

Method: 1. 文本分析：从休谟著作中提取三个表征条件（经验基础、结构化检索、生动性转移）；2. 历史追踪：分析从休谟到贝叶斯认识论和预测处理的形式化演变轨迹；3. 案例研究：以大型语言模型为当代案例，检验其是否满足休谟的三个条件。

Result: 1. 成功识别并论证了休谟因果判断的三个表征条件是其理论的必要组成部分；2. 揭示了从休谟到现代形式化框架的演变中，更新结构被保留而表征条件被抽象化；3. 大型语言模型展示了统计更新如何在不满足休谟三个条件的情况下运作，使原本隐含的假设变得可见。

Conclusion: 休谟的因果判断理论包含三个被忽视的表征条件，这些条件在现代形式化过程中被抽象化。大型语言模型等当代系统通过展示纯统计更新如何运作，使这些背景假设显性化，为理解休谟理论与现代认知科学的关系提供了新视角。

Abstract: Hume's account of causal judgment presupposes three representational conditions: experiential grounding (ideas must trace to impressions), structured retrieval (association must operate through organized networks exceeding pairwise connection), and vivacity transfer (inference must produce felt conviction, not merely updated probability). This paper extracts these conditions from Hume's texts and argues that they are integral to his causal psychology. It then traces their fate through the formalization trajectory from Hume to Bayesian epistemology and predictive processing, showing that later frameworks preserve the updating structure of Hume's insight while abstracting away these further representational conditions. Large language models serve as an illustrative contemporary case: they exhibit a form of statistical updating without satisfying the three conditions, thereby making visible requirements that were previously background assumptions in Hume's framework.

</details>


### [242] [TABQAWORLD: Optimizing Multimodal Reasoning for Multi-Turn Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03393)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Xinyu Wang,Xiaofeng Lin,Peng Lu,Chunhe Wang,Changlun Li,Hanwei Wu,Nan Tang,Elisa Kreiss,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TABQAWORLD：一个免训练的多模态表格推理框架，通过动态切换视觉和文本表示来优化表格状态读取，同时利用表格元数据规划推理轨迹，显著提升准确率并降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮表格推理方法依赖固定的文本序列化来表示表格状态，这会导致表示误差在多轮中累积。虽然表格接地方法可以缓解这个问题，但需要大量推理计算和成本，不适用于实际部署。

Method: 1. 表示层面：采用动作条件化的多模态选择策略，动态切换视觉和文本表示以最大化表格状态读取可靠性。2. 估计层面：利用表格元数据（维度、数据类型、键值）优化逐步推理轨迹，安全规划轨迹并压缩低复杂度动作以减少对话轮次和延迟。

Result: TABQAWORLD实现了最先进的性能，相比基线准确率提升4.87%，相比静态设置准确率提升5.42%，推理延迟降低33.35%。

Conclusion: TABQAWORLD为可靠且高效的表格推理设立了新标准，通过联合优化表示和估计，在免训练的情况下显著提升了推理准确性和效率。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing reasoning capabilities of reasoning models. While multi-turn table reasoning methods have improved reasoning accuracy through tool use and reward modeling, they rely on fixed text serialization for table state readouts. This introduces representation errors in table encoding that significantly accumulate over multiple turns. Such accumulation is alleviated by tabular grounding methods in the expense of inference compute and cost, rendering real world deployment impractical. To address this, we introduce TABQAWORLD, a table reasoning framework that jointly optimizes tabular action through representation and estimation. For representation, TABQAWORLD employs an action-conditioned multimodal selection policy, which dynamically switches between visual and textual representations to maximize table state readout reliability. For estimation, TABQAWORLD optimizes stepwise reasoning trajectory through table metadata including dimension, data types and key values, safely planning trajectory and compressing low-complexity actions to reduce conversation turns and latency. Designed as a training-free framework, empirical evaluations show that TABQAWORLD achieves state-of-the-art performance with 4.87% accuracy improvements over baselines, with 5.42% accuracy gain and 33.35% inference latency reduction over static settings, establishing a new standard for reliable and efficient table reasoning.

</details>


### [243] [Contextual Control without Memory Growth in a Context-Switching Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03479)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于干预的循环架构，通过在共享循环潜在状态上进行干预来实现上下文依赖，无需扩大循环维度或直接输入上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究上下文依赖顺序决策的第三种方法：通过干预共享循环潜在状态来实现上下文依赖，而不是直接输入上下文或增加循环记忆维度。

Method: 引入干预式循环架构：循环核心首先构建共享的预干预潜在状态，然后上下文通过加性的、上下文索引的算子进行干预。在部分可观测的上下文切换顺序决策任务上评估。

Result: 干预模型在主基准测试中表现强劲，无需额外的循环维度。对于任务相关的阶段1结果，干预模型显示出正的上下文条件互信息。

Conclusion: 在共享循环状态上进行干预为上下文控制提供了可行的替代方案，可以替代循环记忆增长，在此设置中有效实现上下文依赖。

Abstract: Context-dependent sequential decision making is commonly addressed either by providing context explicitly as an input or by increasing recurrent memory so that contextual information can be represented internally. We study a third alternative: realizing contextual dependence by intervening on a shared recurrent latent state, without enlarging recurrent dimensionality. To this end, we introduce an intervention-based recurrent architecture in which a recurrent core first constructs a shared pre-intervention latent state, and context then acts through an additive, context-indexed operator. We evaluate this idea on a context-switching sequential decision task under partial observability. We compare three model families: a label-assisted baseline with direct context access, a memory baseline with enlarged recurrent state, and the proposed intervention model, which uses no direct context input to the recurrent core and no memory growth. On the main benchmark, the intervention model performs strongly without additional recurrent dimensions. We also evaluate the models using the conditional mutual information (I(C;O | S)) as a theorem-motivated operational probe of contextual dependence at fixed latent state. For task-relevant phase-1 outcomes, the intervention model exhibits positive conditional contextual information. Together, these results suggest that intervention on a shared recurrent state provides a viable alternative to recurrent memory growth for contextual control in this setting.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Predefined Schemas: TRACE-KG for Context-Enriched Knowledge Graphs from Complex Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03496)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Yang Ba,Yixuan He,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-KG是一个多模态框架，无需预定义本体即可联合构建上下文丰富的知识图谱和推导出的模式，提供结构化限定符和数据驱动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识图谱构建方法存在局限性：基于本体的方法需要昂贵的模式设计和维护成本，而无模式方法通常产生碎片化、缺乏全局组织的图谱，特别是在包含密集上下文信息的长期技术文档中。

Method: 提出TRACE-KG多模态框架，通过结构化限定符捕获条件关系，并使用数据驱动模式组织实体和关系，同时保持对源证据的完全可追溯性。

Result: 实验表明TRACE-KG能够生成结构连贯、可追溯的知识图谱，为基于本体和无模式的构建流程提供了实用替代方案。

Conclusion: TRACE-KG框架在无需预定义本体的情况下，成功构建了上下文丰富的知识图谱和推导模式，平衡了结构一致性和灵活性，是知识图谱构建的有效方法。

Abstract: Knowledge graph construction typically relies either on predefined ontologies or on schema-free extraction. Ontology-driven pipelines enforce consistent typing but require costly schema design and maintenance, whereas schema-free methods often produce fragmented graphs with weak global organization, especially in long technical documents with dense, context-dependent information. We propose TRACE-KG (Text-dRiven schemA for Context-Enriched Knowledge Graphs), a multimodal framework that jointly constructs a context-enriched knowledge graph and an induced schema without assuming a predefined ontology. TRACE-KG captures conditional relations through structured qualifiers and organizes entities and relations using a data-driven schema that serves as a reusable semantic scaffold while preserving full traceability to the source evidence. Experiments show that TRACE-KG produces structurally coherent, traceable knowledge graphs and offers a practical alternative to both ontology-driven and schema-free construction pipelines.

</details>


### [245] [Resource-Conscious Modeling for Next- Day Discharge Prediction Using Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03498)
*Ha Na Cho,Sairam Sutari,Alexander Lopez,Hansen Bow,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了轻量级微调大语言模型与传统文本模型在脊柱择期手术中预测次日出院的效果，发现TF-IDF+LGBM模型在出院类别上表现最佳，而基于Transformer的生成模型表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 及时预测出院对于优化择期脊柱手术单元的床位周转和资源分配至关重要，需要探索轻量级、资源高效的模型在临床预测任务中的可行性。

Method: 比较了13种模型，包括TF-IDF结合XGBoost和LGBM的传统文本模型，以及通过LoRA微调的紧凑型大语言模型（DistilGPT-2、Bio_ClinicalBERT），用于基于术后临床笔记预测次日出院。

Result: TF-IDF结合LGBM模型取得了最佳平衡，出院类别的F1分数为0.47，召回率为0.51，AUC-ROC最高（0.80）。LoRA提高了DistilGPT2的召回率，但总体上基于Transformer和生成模型表现不佳。

Conclusion: 在现实世界不平衡的临床预测任务中，可解释且资源高效的模型可能优于紧凑型大语言模型，传统机器学习方法仍具有实用价值。

Abstract: Timely discharge prediction is essential for optimizing bed turnover and resource allocation in elective spine surgery units. This study evaluates the feasibility of lightweight, fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) and traditional text-based models for predicting next-day discharge using postoperative clinical notes. We compared 13 models, including TF-IDF with XGBoost and LGBM, and compact LLMs (DistilGPT-2, Bio_ClinicalBERT) fine-tuned via LoRA. TF-IDF with LGBM achieved the best balance, with an F1-score of 0.47 for the discharge class, a recall of 0.51, and the highest AUC-ROC (0.80). While LoRA improved recall in DistilGPT2, overall transformer-based and generative models underperformed. These findings suggest interpretable, resource-efficient models may outperform compact LLMs in real-world, imbalanced clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [246] [BioAlchemy: Distilling Biological Literature into Reasoning-Ready Reinforcement Learning Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03506)
*Brian Hsu,Ozan Gökdemir,Carlo Siebenschuh,Bruce Parrello,Neil Getty,Thomas S. Brettin,Rick L. Stevens,Ian T. Foster,Nicholas Chia,Arvind Ramanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioAlchemy是一个从生物学研究文本中提取多样化、可验证问答对的管道，创建了包含34.5万个科学推理问题的数据集，并通过强化学习对齐现代生物学研究主题分布，提升了生物学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模推理数据集中的生物学问题与现生物学研究主题分布不匹配，这种主题不平衡可能影响性能。同时，从生物学研究文本中提取具有挑战性和可验证的研究问题的方法在应用强化学习提升生物学研究任务性能方面是重要但未充分发展的要素。

Method: 引入BioAlchemy管道，从生物学研究文本语料库中获取多样化的可验证问答对。创建了BioAlchemy-345K训练数据集，包含超过34.5万个生物学科学推理问题。通过强化学习将数据集对齐到现代科学生物学的主题分布。

Result: 开发了BioAlchemist-8B模型，相比其基础推理模型在生物学基准测试上提升了9.12%。结果表明该方法能有效增强生物学科学推理能力。

Conclusion: BioAlchemy方法通过创建与现生物学研究主题分布对齐的高质量数据集，并结合强化学习，显著提升了生物学推理模型的性能，为解决生物学推理模型发展滞后问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Despite the large corpus of biology training text, the impact of reasoning models on biological research generally lags behind math and coding. In this work, we show that biology questions from current large-scale reasoning datasets do not align well with modern research topic distributions in biology, and that this topic imbalance may negatively affect performance. In addition, we find that methods for extracting challenging and verifiable research problems from biology research text are a critical yet underdeveloped ingredient in applying reinforcement learning for better performance on biology research tasks. We introduce BioAlchemy, a pipeline for sourcing a diverse set of verifiable question-and-answer pairs from a scientific corpus of biology research text. We curate BioAlchemy-345K, a training dataset containing over 345K scientific reasoning problems in biology. Then, we demonstrate how aligning our dataset to the topic distribution of modern scientific biology can be used with reinforcement learning to improve reasoning performance. Finally, we present BioAlchemist-8B, which improves over its base reasoning model by 9.12% on biology benchmarks. These results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach for developing stronger scientific reasoning capabilities in biology. The BioAlchemist-8B model is available at: https://huggingface.co/BioAlchemy.

</details>


### [247] [ActionNex: A Virtual Outage Manager for Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03512)
*Zhenfeng Lin,Haoji Hu,Ming Hao,Xuchao Zhang,Ryan Zhang,Junhao Li,Ze Li,Oleg Kulygin,Chetan Bansal,Hatay Tuna,Murali Chintalapati,Sheila Jiang,Salman Zafar,Angie Anderson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActionNex是一个生产级智能系统，用于大规模云运维中的故障管理，通过多模态信号处理、分层记忆系统和推理代理提供端到端故障协助，包括实时更新、知识提炼和最佳行动建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模云运维中的故障管理仍然高度依赖人工，需要快速分类、跨团队协调和在部分可观测性下做出经验驱动的决策。现有系统缺乏端到端的自动化支持。

Method: 系统接收多模态运维信号（故障内容、遥测数据、人工通信），将其压缩为关键事件表示状态转换。采用分层记忆子系统：从操作手册和历史执行中提炼的长期KCA知识、先前故障的情景记忆、实时上下文的工作记忆。推理代理将当前关键事件与前提条件对齐，检索相关记忆并生成可操作建议，执行的人工操作作为隐式反馈信号实现持续自我进化。

Result: 在8个真实Azure故障（800万token，4000个关键事件）上评估，使用两个互补的真实行动集，达到71.4%的精确率和52.8-54.8%的召回率。系统已在生产中试点并获得积极早期反馈。

Conclusion: ActionNex展示了在复杂云运维环境中实现人机混合系统的可行性，通过分层记忆和推理代理提供有效的故障管理协助，并具备持续自我进化的能力。

Abstract: Outage management in large-scale cloud operations remains heavily manual, requiring rapid triage, cross-team coordination, and experience-driven decisions under partial observability. We present \textbf{ActionNex}, a production-grade agentic system that supports end-to-end outage assistance, including real-time updates, knowledge distillation, and role- and stage-conditioned next-best action recommendations. ActionNex ingests multimodal operational signals (e.g., outage content, telemetry, and human communications) and compresses them into critical events that represent meaningful state transitions. It couples this perception layer with a hierarchical memory subsystem: long-term Key-Condition-Action (KCA) knowledge distilled from playbooks and historical executions, episodic memory of prior outages, and working memory of the live context. A reasoning agent aligns current critical events to preconditions, retrieves relevant memories, and generates actionable recommendations; executed human actions serve as an implicit feedback signal to enable continual self-evolution in a human-agent hybrid system. We evaluate ActionNex on eight real Azure outages (8M tokens, 4,000 critical events) using two complementary ground-truth action sets, achieving 71.4\% precision and 52.8-54.8\% recall. The system has been piloted in production and has received positive early feedback.

</details>


### [248] [Structural Rigidity and the 57-Token Predictive Window: A Physical Framework for Inference-Layer Governability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03524)
*Gregory M. Ruddell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前AI安全依赖行为监控和后训练对齐，但实证测量显示多数指令调优模型没有可检测的预承诺信号。本文提出基于能量的治理框架，连接Transformer推理动态与约束满足模型，在七模型队列中识别出特定条件下的预承诺窗口，建立五类推理行为分类，并证明规则违反和幻觉是不同的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全主要依赖行为监控和后训练对齐，但这些方法在大多数指令调优模型中无法检测到预承诺信号。需要建立可测量的推理层治理框架，以评估自主AI系统的部署风险。

Method: 提出基于能量的治理框架，将Transformer推理动态连接到约束满足的神经计算模型。使用轨迹张力(rho = ||a|| / ||v||)作为关键指标，在七模型队列中分析五个几何机制。引入五类推理行为分类：权威带、晚期信号、倒置、平坦和脚手架选择性。

Result: 在Phi-3-mini-4k-instruct模型的贪婪解码算术约束探测中识别出57个token的预承诺窗口，但这是模型、任务和配置特定的。七模型中仅一种配置显示预测性信号，其他显示静默失效、晚期检测、倒置动态或平坦几何。事实幻觉在72个测试条件下无预测信号。

Conclusion: 规则违反和幻觉是不同的失效模式，需要不同的检测方法。内部几何监控仅在存在阻力时有效，事实混淆检测需要外部验证机制。该工作为推理层可治理性提供了可测量框架，并引入了评估自主AI系统部署风险的分类法。

Abstract: Current AI safety relies on behavioral monitoring and post-training alignment, yet empirical measurement shows these approaches produce no detectable pre-commitment signal in a majority of instruction-tuned models tested. We present an energy-based governance framework connecting transformer inference dynamics to constraint-satisfaction models of neural computation, and apply it to a seven-model cohort across five geometric regimes.
  Using trajectory tension (rho = ||a|| / ||v||), we identify a 57-token pre-commitment window in Phi-3-mini-4k-instruct under greedy decoding on arithmetic constraint probes. This result is model-specific, task-specific, and configuration-specific, demonstrating that pre-commitment signals can exist but are not universal.
  We introduce a five-regime taxonomy of inference behavior: Authority Band, Late Signal, Inverted, Flat, and Scaffold-Selective. Energy asymmetry (Σ\r{ho}_misaligned / Σ\r{ho}_aligned) serves as a unifying metric of structural rigidity across these regimes.
  Across seven models, only one configuration exhibits a predictive signal prior to commitment; all others show silent failure, late detection, inverted dynamics, or flat geometry.
  We further demonstrate that factual hallucination produces no predictive signal across 72 test conditions, consistent with spurious attractor settling in the absence of a trained world-model constraint.
  These results establish that rule violation and hallucination are distinct failure modes with different detection requirements. Internal geometry monitoring is effective only where resistance exists; detection of factual confabulation requires external verification mechanisms.
  This work provides a measurable framework for inference-layer governability and introduces a taxonomy for evaluating deployment risk in autonomous AI systems.

</details>


### [249] [Explainable Model Routing for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03527)
*Mika Okamoto,Ansel Kaplan Erol,Mark Riedl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Topaz框架为智能体路由系统引入可审计性，通过技能分析、可追踪路由算法和自然语言解释，让开发者能够理解和调整成本与质量的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体工作流的路由架构只关注性能优化，没有记录模型能力与成本之间的权衡。缺乏明确的决策依据，开发者无法区分智能效率（为合适任务使用专门模型）和预算驱动模型选择导致的潜在失败。

Method: Topaz框架包含三个核心组件：1) 基于技能的分析，将多样化基准测试中的性能合成为细粒度能力档案；2) 完全可追踪的路由算法，使用基于预算和多目标优化，产生技能匹配分数与成本权衡的清晰痕迹；3) 面向开发者的解释，将这些痕迹转化为自然语言，允许用户审计系统逻辑并迭代调整成本-质量权衡。

Result: Topaz通过使路由决策可解释，让用户能够理解、信任并有意义地引导路由智能体系统，解决了当前路由架构缺乏可审计性的问题。

Conclusion: Topaz框架为智能体路由系统引入了形式化的可审计性，通过可解释的路由决策，使开发者能够理解、信任并有效调整智能体工作流中的成本-质量权衡。

Abstract: Modern agentic workflows decompose complex tasks into specialized subtasks and route them to diverse models to minimize cost without sacrificing quality. However, current routing architectures focus exclusively on performance optimization, leaving underlying trade-offs between model capability and cost unrecorded. Without clear rationale, developers cannot distinguish between intelligent efficiency -- using specialized models for appropriate tasks -- and latent failures caused by budget-driven model selection. We present Topaz, a framework that introduces formal auditability to agentic routing. Topaz replaces silent model assignments with an inherently interpretable router that incorporates three components: (i) skill-based profiling that synthesizes performance across diverse benchmarks into granular capability profiles (ii) fully traceable routing algorithms that utilize budget-based and multi-objective optimization to produce clear traces of how skill-match scores were weighed against costs, and (iii) developer-facing explanations that translate these traces into natural language, allowing users to audit system logic and iteratively tune the cost-quality tradeoff. By making routing decisions interpretable, Topaz enables users to understand, trust, and meaningfully steer routed agentic systems.

</details>


### [250] [Automated Analysis of Global AI Safety Initiatives: A Taxonomy-Driven LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03533)
*Takayuki Semitsu,Naoto Kiribuchi,Kengo Zenitani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自动化交叉比对框架，使用AI安全活动分类法比较政策文档，评估LLM在政策分析中的稳定性和有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统化比较AI安全政策文档，评估大语言模型在政策分析中的表现，了解模型选择对分析结果的影响

Method: 基于AI安全活动分类法构建自动化交叉比对框架，使用5个LLM对10个公开政策文档进行交叉比对，通过热图可视化相似度得分，并进行专家人工评估验证

Result: 模型选择显著影响交叉比对结果，某些文档对在不同模型间存在高度分歧；专家评估显示高一致性，但模型得分与人工判断仍有差异

Conclusion: 该框架支持政策文档的比较分析，但需注意模型选择的影响，模型结果与人工判断的差异表明需要进一步改进

Abstract: We present an automated crosswalk framework that compares an AI safety policy document pair under a shared taxonomy of activities. Using the activity categories defined in Activity Map on AI Safety as fixed aspects, the system extracts and maps relevant activities, then produces for each aspect a short summary for each document, a brief comparison, and a similarity score. We assess the stability and validity of LLM-based crosswalk analysis across public policy documents. Using five large language models, we perform crosswalks on ten publicly available documents and visualize mean similarity scores with a heatmap. The results show that model choice substantially affects the crosswalk outcomes, and that some document pairs yield high disagreements across models. A human evaluation by three experts on two document pairs shows high inter-annotator agreement, while model scores still differ from human judgments. These findings support comparative inspection of policy documents.

</details>


### [251] [Towards the AI Historian: Agentic Information Extraction from Primary Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03553)
*Lorenz Hufe,Niclas Griesshaber,Gavin Greif,Sebastian Oliver Eck,Philip Torr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Chronos AI历史学家系统的首个模块，让历史学家通过自然语言交互将原始文献图像扫描转换为数据，支持定制化工作流程和模型评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在历史研究中的应用有限，缺乏专门为历史学家设计的解决方案。历史研究需要处理异构的原始文献，而现有AI工具无法满足历史学家的特定需求。

Method: 开发Chronos AI历史学家系统的首个模块，允许历史学家通过自然语言交互将图像扫描转换为数据。系统不采用固定的视觉语言模型提取流程，而是支持定制工作流程、评估AI模型性能，并通过与Chronos代理的自然语言交互迭代优化工作流程。

Result: 推出了Chronos系统的第一个开源模块，历史研究人员可以在自己的原始文献上使用该系统，实现图像扫描到数据的转换。

Conclusion: 该模块为历史研究提供了专门的AI工具，通过自然语言交互和可定制的工作流程，解决了历史学家在处理异构原始文献时面临的挑战，推动了AI在历史研究中的应用。

Abstract: AI is supporting, accelerating, and automating scientific discovery across a diverse set of fields. However, AI adoption in historical research remains limited due to the lack of solutions designed for historians. In this technical progress report, we introduce the first module of Chronos, an AI Historian under development. This module enables historians to convert image scans of primary sources into data through natural-language interactions. Rather than imposing a fixed extraction pipeline powered by a vision-language model (VLM), it allows historians to adapt workflows for heterogeneous source corpora, evaluate the performance of AI models on specific tasks, and iteratively refine workflows through natural-language interaction with the Chronos agent. The module is open-source and ready to be used by historical researchers on their own sources.

</details>


### [252] [When Do Hallucinations Arise? A Graph Perspective on the Evolution of Path Reuse and Path Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03557)
*Xinnan Dai,Kai Yang,Cheng Luo,Shenglai Zeng,Kai Guo,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将LLM的推理幻觉建模为图搜索过程，揭示了路径重用和路径压缩两种机制导致幻觉产生


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM中推理幻觉现象普遍存在，但解码器Transformer产生这些幻觉的机制仍不清楚。需要理解为什么LLM会产生流畅但无根据的结论，这些结论违反了上下文或事实知识。

Method: 将下一个token预测建模为图搜索过程：实体作为节点，学习到的转换形成边。上下文推理是采样子图上的约束搜索（内在推理），而无上下文查询则依赖于底层图中的记忆结构（外在推理）。

Result: 发现推理幻觉源于两种基本机制：1) 路径重用：早期训练中记忆的知识覆盖上下文约束；2) 路径压缩：后期训练中频繁遍历的多步路径坍缩为捷径边。这些机制为LLM中的推理幻觉提供了统一解释。

Conclusion: 路径重用和路径压缩机制共同解释了LLM中推理幻觉的产生，并与下游应用中观察到的已知行为相关联，为理解Transformer的推理失败提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Reasoning hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) often appear as fluent yet unsupported conclusions that violate either the given context or underlying factual knowledge. Although such failures are widely observed, the mechanisms by which decoder-only Transformers produce them remain poorly understood. We model next-token prediction as a graph search process over an underlying graph, where entities correspond to nodes and learned transitions form edges. From this perspective, contextual reasoning is a constrained search over a sampled subgraph (intrinsic reasoning), while context-free queries rely on memorized structures in the underlying graph (extrinsic reasoning). We show that reasoning hallucinations arise from two fundamental mechanisms: \textbf{Path Reuse}, where memorized knowledge overrides contextual constraints during early training, and \textbf{Path Compression}, where frequently traversed multi-step paths collapse into shortcut edges in later training. Together, these mechanisms provide a unified explanation for reasoning hallucinations in LLMs and connected to well-known behaviors observed in downstream applications.

</details>


### [253] [When Adaptive Rewards Hurt: Causal Probing and the Switching-Stability Dilemma in LLM-Guided LEO Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03562)
*Yuanhang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 自适应奖励设计在LEO卫星调度中面临切换-稳定性困境：静态权重优于动态调整，因为PPO需要准静态奖励信号。因果探测揭示反直觉杠杆效应，MLP表现优于LLM，后者因权重振荡而崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自适应奖励设计在LEO卫星多波束调度中的效果，直觉认为动态调整的奖励权重应优于静态权重，但需要系统验证这一假设。

Method: 1) 测试静态与动态奖励权重性能；2) 引入单变量因果探测方法，独立扰动每个奖励项±20%并测量PPO响应；3) 评估四种MDP架构变体：固定权重、基于规则、学习MLP、微调LLM。

Result: 1) 静态权重(342.1 Mbps)优于动态权重(103.3±96.8 Mbps)；2) 因果探测发现反直觉杠杆：切换惩罚+20%带来极区切换+157 Mbps、冷热区域+130 Mbps；3) MLP在已知/新流量模式下表现最佳(357.9/325.2 Mbps)，微调LLM因权重振荡崩溃(45.3±43.0 Mbps)。

Conclusion: PPO需要准静态奖励信号，动态权重调整会破坏收敛；因果探测揭示人类专家无法发现的奖励杠杆；LLM在DRL集成中价值有限（仅自然语言理解），简单方法更有效；为通信系统LLM-DRL集成提供实证路线图。

Abstract: Adaptive reward design for deep reinforcement learning (DRL) in multi-beam LEO satellite scheduling is motivated by the intuition that regime-aware reward weights should outperform static ones. We systematically test this intuition and uncover a switching-stability dilemma: near-constant reward weights (342.1 Mbps) outperform carefully-tuned dynamic weights (103.3+/-96.8 Mbps) because PPO requires a quasistationary reward signal for value function convergence. Weight adaptation-regardless of quality-degrades performance by repeatedly restarting convergence. To understand why specific weights matter, we introduce a single-variable causal probing method that independently perturbs each reward term by +/-20% and measures PPO response after 50k steps. Probing reveals counterintuitive leverage: a +20% increase in the switching penalty yields +157 Mbps for polar handover and +130 Mbps for hot-cold regimes-findings inaccessible to human experts or trained MLPs without systematic probing. We evaluate four MDP architect variants (fixed, rule-based, learned MLP, finetuned LLM) across known and novel traffic regimes. The MLP achieves 357.9 Mbps on known regimes and 325.2 Mbps on novel regimes, while the fine-tuned LLM collapses to 45.3+/-43.0 Mbps due to weight oscillation rather than lack of domain knowledge-output consistency, not knowledge, is the binding constraint. Our findings provide an empirically-grounded roadmap for LLM-DRL integration in communication systems, identifying where LLMs add irreplaceable value (natural language intent understanding) versus where simpler methods suffice.

</details>


### [254] [Personality Requires Struggle: Three Regimes of the Baldwin Effect in Neuroevolved Chess Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03565)
*Diego Armando Resendez Prado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨终身学习能否增加而非减少行为多样性，发现赫布可塑性在进化过程中会逆转其对行为方差的影响：初期压缩多样性，后期通过想象力放大感知差异，从而扩展多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统理论认为可塑性通过缓冲环境噪声来减少变异，但本研究想探究终身学习是否能在进化时间尺度上扩展行为多样性，而非压缩它。

Method: 使用NEAT进化的神经网络模块（8个）、赫布游戏内可塑性机制、带有想象力的期望域信号链的象棋智能体，在10个种子条件下进行实验。

Result: 发现方差交叉现象：赫布开启初期跨种子方差低于关闭状态，但在第34代后反超。行为出现结构化分歧：62%的走法分歧、不同的开局库、棋子偏好和游戏时长。识别出三种机制：探索型、彩票型和透明型。

Conclusion: 可塑性对行为多样性的影响在进化过程中会发生逆转，想象力放大了进化感知差异，形成了可复现的行为特征。自对弈系统可能通过消除异质性而系统性抑制行为多样性。

Abstract: Can lifetime learning expand behavioral diversity over evolutionary time, rather than collapsing it? Prior theory predicts that plasticity reduces variance by buffering organisms against environmental noise. We test this in a competitive domain: chess agents with eight NEAT-evolved neural modules, Hebbian within-game plasticity, and a desirability-domain signal chain with imagination. Across 10~seeds per Hebbian condition, a variance crossover emerges: Hebbian ON starts with lower cross-seed variance than OFF, then surpasses it at generation~34. The crossover trend is monotonic (\r{ho} = 0.91, p < 10^{-6): plasticity's effect on behavioral variance reverses over evolutionary time, initially compressing diversity (consistent with prior predictions) then expanding it as evolved Perception differences are amplified through imagination -- a feedback loop that mutation alone cannot sustain.
  The result is structured behavioral divergence: evolved agents select different moves on the same positions (62\% disagreement), develop distinct opening repertoires, piece preferences, and game lengths. These are not different sampling policies -- they are reproducible behavioral signatures (ICC > 0.8) with interpretable signal chain configurations. Three regimes appear depending on opponent type: exploration (Hebbian ON, heterogeneous opponent), lottery (Hebbian OFF, elitism lock-in), and transparent (same-model opponent, brain self-erasure). The transparent regime generates a falsifiable prediction: self-play systems may systematically suppress behavioral diversity by eliminating the heterogeneity that personality requires.
  \textbf{Keywords: Baldwin Effect, neuroevolution, NEAT, Hebbian learning, chess, cognitive architecture, personality emergence, imagination

</details>


### [255] [Selective Forgetting for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03571)
*Tuan Le,Wei Qian,Mengdi Huai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出针对大型推理模型的知识遗忘框架，选择性移除敏感推理组件同时保持通用推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过结构化思维链生成答案，容易在中间推理步骤泄露敏感信息（如版权和隐私内容），现有遗忘方法主要针对最终答案，可能损害模型的整体推理能力

Method: 使用多LLM结合检索增强生成分析思维链轨迹，识别需要遗忘的相关片段，用保持逻辑结构的良性占位符替换，并引入特征替换遗忘损失函数

Result: 在合成和医疗数据集上的广泛实验验证了所提方法的理想特性

Conclusion: 该框架能够精确遗忘目标知识同时保持通用推理能力的完整性，解决了LRM遗忘的关键挑战

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) generate structured chains of thought (CoTs) before producing final answers, making them especially vulnerable to knowledge leakage through intermediate reasoning steps. Yet, the memorization of sensitive information in the training data such as copyrighted and private content has led to ethical and legal concerns. To address these issues, selective forgetting (also known as machine unlearning) has emerged as a potential remedy for LRMs. However, existing unlearning methods primarily target final answers and may degrade the overall reasoning ability of LRMs after forgetting. Additionally, directly applying unlearning on the entire CoTs could degrade the general reasoning capabilities. The key challenge for LRM unlearning lies in achieving precise unlearning of targeted knowledge while preserving the integrity of general reasoning capabilities. To bridge this gap, we in this paper propose a novel LRM unlearning framework that selectively removes sensitive reasoning components while preserving general reasoning capabilities. Our approach leverages multiple LLMs with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to analyze CoT traces, identify forget-relevant segments, and replace them with benign placeholders that maintain logical structure. We also introduce a new feature replacement unlearning loss for LRMs, which can simultaneously suppress the probability of generating forgotten content while reinforcing structurally valid replacements. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and medical datasets verify the desired properties of our proposed method.

</details>


### [256] [Rashomon Memory: Towards Argumentation-Driven Retrieval for Multi-Perspective Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03588)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Rashomon记忆架构，让并行目标导向的智能体根据各自优先级编码经验，通过论证在查询时协商，支持冲突解释共存而非强制统一


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在长期运作中积累的经验服务于多个并发目标，经常需要维护对同一事件相互冲突的解释。现有记忆架构假设单一正确编码，无法处理这种多视角需求

Method: Rashomon记忆架构：并行目标导向智能体根据各自优先级编码经验，每个视角维护自己的本体和知识图谱。检索时，各视角提出解释，使用不对称领域知识相互批判，通过Dung论证语义学确定哪些提案保留

Result: 概念验证显示检索模式（选择、组合、冲突浮现）从攻击图拓扑中涌现，冲突浮现模式让系统报告真实分歧而非强制解决，使决策者能直接看到底层解释冲突

Conclusion: Rashomon记忆架构支持冲突解释共存，论证图本身提供解释性记录，显示哪些解释被选择、哪些被考虑、基于什么理由被拒绝，为多视角AI系统提供新范式

Abstract: AI agents operating over extended time horizons accumulate experiences that serve multiple concurrent goals, and must often maintain conflicting interpretations of the same events. A concession during a client negotiation encodes as a ``trust-building investment'' for one strategic goal and a ``contractual liability'' for another. Current memory architectures assume a single correct encoding, or at best support multiple views over unified storage. We propose Rashomon Memory: an architecture where parallel goal-conditioned agents encode experiences according to their priorities and negotiate at query time through argumentation. Each perspective maintains its own ontology and knowledge graph. At retrieval, perspectives propose interpretations, critique each other's proposals using asymmetric domain knowledge, and Dung's argumentation semantics determines which proposals survive. The resulting attack graph is itself an explanation: it records which interpretation was selected, which alternatives were considered, and on what grounds they were rejected. We present a proof-of-concept showing that retrieval modes (selection, composition, conflict surfacing) emerge from attack graph topology, and that the conflict surfacing mode, where the system reports genuine disagreement rather than forcing resolution, lets decision-makers see the underlying interpretive conflict directly.

</details>


### [257] [Entropy and Attention Dynamics in Small Language Models: A Trace-Level Structural Analysis on the TruthfulQA Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03589)
*Adeyemi Adeseye,Aisvarya Adeseye,Hannu Tenhunen,Jouni Isoaho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对小型语言模型在TruthfulQA数据集上的熵和注意力动态进行追踪分析，发现模型可分为确定性、探索性和平衡性三类，每类具有不同的内部行为模式，这些模式影响模型输出的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在资源受限环境中部署增多，但存在自信的错误预测和不稳定输出，现有评估方法仅关注最终准确率或幻觉率，缺乏对内部行为如何影响输出的解释，特别是熵演化、注意力分布和隐藏表示如何影响不确定性、逻辑不一致和错误信息传播。

Method: 使用TruthfulQA数据集对四个1B-1.7B参数的小型语言模型进行追踪分析，包括：token级输出熵、注意力熵、头分散度和隐藏状态表示分析，识别模型内部行为模式。

Result: 发现三种模型分类：确定性模型（DeepSeek-1.5B和LLaMA-1B）输出熵随时间减少；探索性模型（Gemma-1B）熵增加；平衡模型（Qwen-1.7B）熵中等且稳定。每组模型具有不同的隐藏状态移动和注意力分散模式。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型的真实性源于结构化的熵和注意力动态，监控和优化这些内部不确定性模式可以指导设计更可靠、幻觉感知和特定应用的小型语言模型。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) have been increasingly deployed in edge devices and other resource-constrained settings. However, these models make confident mispredictions and produce unstable output, making them risky for factual and decision-critical tasks. Current evaluation methodology relies on final accuracy or hallucination rates without explaining how internal model behavior affects outputs. Specifically, how entropy evolves during decoding, how attention is distributed across layers, and how hidden representations contribute to uncertainty, logical inconsistencies, and misinformation propagation are often overlooked. Consequently, this study introduces a trace-level analysis of entropy and attention dynamics in SLMs evaluated with the TruthfulQA dataset. Four models with parameter ranges of 1B-1.7B parameters were examined via token-level output entropy, attention entropy, head dispersion, and hidden-state representation. The results reflect three model classifications by entropy patterns. Deterministic models (DeepSeek-1.5B and LLaMA-1B): output entropy decreases over time. Exploratory models (Gemma-1B): with increasing entropy, and balanced models (Qwen-1.7B): have moderate and stable entropy. Also, each group has distinctively different hidden-state movement and attention dispersion patterns. The analysis demonstrates that truthfulness in SLMs emerges from structured entropy and attention dynamics. Monitoring and optimizing these internal uncertainty patterns can guide the design of a more reliable, hallucination-aware, and application-specific edge SLMs.

</details>


### [258] [A Multimodal Foundation Model of Spatial Transcriptomics and Histology for Biological Discovery and Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03630)
*Jinxi Xiang,Siyu Hou,Yuchen Li,Ryan Quinton,Xiaoming Zhang,Feyisope Eweje,Xiangde Luo,Yijiang Chen,Zhe Li,Colin Bergstrom,Ted Kim,Sierra Willens,Francesca Maria Olguin,Matthew Abikenari,Andrew Heider,Sanjeeth Rajaram,Joel Neal,Maximilian Diehn,Xiang Zhou,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STORM是一个空间转录组学基础模型，整合组织学图像和基因表达数据，能够从H&E图像预测空间基因表达，并提升免疫治疗反应预测和预后评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高、通量低，而H&E染色虽提供丰富形态学信息但缺乏分子分辨率。需要一种能够桥接成像和组学的方法，实现从组织学图像预测分子信息。

Method: 开发STORM基础模型，在18个器官的120万个空间转录组学图谱上训练，匹配组织学数据。采用分层架构整合形态特征、基因表达和空间上下文信息。

Result: STORM在11种肿瘤类型中从H&E图像预测空间基因表达方面优于现有方法，增强空间域发现，产生生物学一致的组织图谱。在7,245名患者的23个独立队列中显著改善免疫治疗反应预测和预后评估。

Conclusion: STORM提供了一个可扩展的框架，用于空间信息发现和临床精准医学，能够桥接成像和组学，实现从常规组织学图像中提取分子信息。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables gene expression mapping within anatomical context but remains costly and low-throughput. Hematoxylin and eosin (H\&E) staining offers rich morphology yet lacks molecular resolution. We present \textbf{\ours} (\textbf{S}patial \textbf{T}ranscriptomics and hist\textbf{O}logy \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{M}odel), a foundation model trained on 1.2 million spatially resolved transcriptomic profiles with matched histology across 18 organs. Using a hierarchical architecture integrating morphological features, gene expression, and spatial context, STORM bridges imaging and omics through robust molecular--morphological representations. STORM enhances spatial domain discovery, producing biologically coherent tissue maps, and outperforms existing methods in predicting spatial gene expression from H\&E images across 11 tumor types. The model is platform-agnostic, performing consistently across Visium, Xenium, Visium HD, and CosMx. Applied to 23 independent cohorts comprising 7,245 patients, STORM significantly improves immunotherapy response prediction and prognostication over established biomarkers, providing a scalable framework for spatially informed discovery and clinical precision medicine.

</details>


### [259] [Single-agent vs. Multi-agents for Automated Video Analysis of On-Screen Collaborative Learning Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03631)
*Likai Peng,Shihui Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了闭源和开源视觉语言模型在单智能体与多智能体设置下，基于ICAP框架对协作学习场景中屏幕录像进行自动编码的性能，提出了两种多智能体框架并验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕学习行为能揭示学生在学习过程中的信息寻求、使用和创造方式，分析屏幕行为参与度对捕捉学生的认知和协作过程至关重要。视觉语言模型的发展为自动化处理多模态视频数据分析提供了新机会，可减少传统手动编码的繁重工作。

Method: 比较了领先的闭源VLM（Claude-3.7-Sonnet, GPT-4.1）和开源VLM（Qwen2.5-VL-72B）在单智能体和多智能体设置下的性能。提出了两种多智能体框架：1）基于工作流的三智能体系统，通过场景分割和光标提示的VLM检测屏幕行为并进行证据验证；2）受ReAct启发的自主决策多智能体系统，迭代进行推理、工具操作（分割/分类/验证）和观察驱动的自我修正。

Result: 实验结果表明，两种提出的多智能体框架都取得了可行性能，在场景和行为检测任务上优于单VLM。工作流智能体在场景检测上表现最佳，自主决策多智能体在行为检测上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了基于VLM的多智能体系统在视频分析中的有效性，为多模态数据分析提供了一个可扩展的框架。

Abstract: On-screen learning behavior provides valuable insights into how students seek, use, and create information during learning. Analyzing on-screen behavioral engagement is essential for capturing students' cognitive and collaborative processes. The recent development of Vision Language Models (VLMs) offers new opportunities to automate the labor-intensive manual coding often required for multimodal video data analysis. In this study, we compared the performance of both leading closed-source VLMs (Claude-3.7-Sonnet, GPT-4.1) and open-source VLM (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) in single- and multi-agent settings for automated coding of screen recordings in collaborative learning contexts based on the ICAP framework. In particular, we proposed and compared two multi-agent frameworks: 1) a three-agent workflow multi-agent system (MAS) that segments screen videos by scene and detects on-screen behaviors using cursor-informed VLM prompting with evidence-based verification; 2) an autonomous-decision MAS inspired by ReAct that iteratively interleaves reasoning, tool-like operations (segmentation/ classification/ validation), and observation-driven self-correction to produce interpretable on-screen behavior labels. Experimental results demonstrated that the two proposed MAS frameworks achieved viable performance, outperforming the single VLMs in scene and action detection tasks. It is worth noting that the workflow-based agent achieved best on scene detection, and the autonomous-decision MAS achieved best on action detection. This study demonstrates the effectiveness of VLM-based Multi-agent System for video analysis and contributes a scalable framework for multimodal data analytics.

</details>


### [260] [Beyond Retrieval: Modeling Confidence Decay and Deterministic Agentic Platforms in Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03656)
*XinYu Zhao,ChengYou Li,XiangBao Meng,Kai Zhang,XiaoDong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出从基于RAG的概率性GEO转向确定性多智能体意图路由的新范式，通过数学建模语义熵漂移、引入同构归因回归模型和确定性智能体交接协议，显著降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于检索增强生成的生成引擎优化存在概率性幻觉和"零点击"悖论问题，无法建立可持续的商业信任，需要转向更确定性的方法。

Method: 1) 数学建模语义熵漂移以描述LLM置信度曲线的动态衰减；2) 引入同构归因回归模型量化黑盒商业引擎的优化价值；3) 设计确定性智能体交接协议，构建智能体信任代理生态系统，让LLM仅作为意图路由器而非最终答案生成器。

Result: 在Yishu Technology的EasyNote工业AI会议纪要产品中验证，通过DAH协议将"无限画布上的知识图谱映射"意图直接路由到专用专有智能体，垂直任务幻觉率降低至接近零。

Conclusion: 这项工作为下一代生成引擎优化建立了基础理论框架，为有序、确定性的人机协作生态系统铺平了道路。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) is rapidly reshaping digital marketing paradigms in the era of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, current GEO strategies predominantly rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), which inherently suffers from probabilistic hallucinations and the "zero-click" paradox, failing to establish sustainable commercial trust. In this paper, we systematically deconstruct the probabilistic flaws of existing RAG-based GEO and propose a paradigm shift towards deterministic multi-agent intent routing. First, we mathematically formulate Semantic Entropy Drift (SED) to model the dynamic decay of confidence curves in LLMs over continuous temporal and contextual perturbations. To rigorously quantify optimization value in black-box commercial engines, we introduce the Isomorphic Attribution Regression (IAR) model, leveraging a Multi-Agent System (MAS) probe with strict human-in-the-loop physical isolation to enforce hallucination penalties. Furthermore, we architect the Deterministic Agent Handoff (DAH) protocol, conceptualizing an Agentic Trust Brokerage (ATB) ecosystem where LLMs function solely as intent routers rather than final answer generators. We empirically validate this architecture using EasyNote, an industrial AI meeting minutes product by Yishu Technology. By routing the intent of "knowledge graph mapping on an infinite canvas" directly to its specialized proprietary agent via DAH, we demonstrate the reduction of vertical task hallucination rates to near zero. This work establishes a foundational theoretical framework for next-generation GEO and paves the way for a well-ordered, deterministic human-AI collaboration ecosystem.

</details>


### [261] [TableVision: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Spatially Grounded Reasoning over Complex Hierarchical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03660)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Lu Dai,Hanqing Wang,Zhuoyu Li,Wenbin Dai,Yanzong Zheng,Zhenggang Xia,Junyong Lin,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TableVision：针对结构化表格推理的大规模轨迹感知基准，通过显式空间约束解决MLLMs在复杂表格中的感知瓶颈问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）取得进展，但在处理具有层次布局的复杂表格时，推理性能仍然有限。研究发现存在"感知瓶颈"：随着任务复杂度增加，涉及的离散视觉区域数量不成比例增长，导致"感知过载"，MLLMs在隐式生成过程中难以维持准确的空间注意力。

Method: 提出TableVision基准，将表格任务分为三个认知层次（感知、推理、分析）和13个子类别。采用基于渲染的确定性接地管道，将多步逻辑推导与像素级空间真值显式耦合，包含6,799个高保真推理轨迹。通过两阶段解耦框架和显式空间约束来提升MLLMs的推理能力。

Result: 诊断探测支持的实证结果表明，显式空间约束显著恢复了MLLMs的推理潜力。两阶段解耦框架在测试集上实现了12.3%的整体准确率提升。

Conclusion: TableVision为文档理解中感知与逻辑的协同提供了严格的测试平台和新视角，通过显式空间接地解决了MLLMs在复杂表格推理中的感知瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Structured tables are essential for conveying high-density information in professional domains such as finance, healthcare, and scientific research. Despite the progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), reasoning performance remains limited for complex tables with hierarchical layouts. In this paper, we identify a critical Perception Bottleneck through quantitative analysis. We find that as task complexity scales, the number of involved discrete visual regions increases disproportionately. This processing density leads to an internal "Perceptual Overload," where MLLMs struggle to maintain accurate spatial attention during implicit generation. To address this bottleneck, we introduce TableVision, a large-scale, trajectory-aware benchmark designed for spatially grounded reasoning. TableVision stratifies tabular tasks into three cognitive levels (Perception, Reasoning, and Analysis) across 13 sub-categories. By utilizing a rendering-based deterministic grounding pipeline, the dataset explicitly couples multi-step logical deductions with pixel-perfect spatial ground truths, comprising 6,799 high-fidelity reasoning trajectories. Our empirical results, supported by diagnostic probing, demonstrate that explicit spatial constraints significantly recover the reasoning potential of MLLMs. Furthermore, our two-stage decoupled framework achieves a robust 12.3% overall accuracy improvement on the test set. TableVision provides a rigorous testbed and a fresh perspective on the synergy between perception and logic in document understanding.

</details>


### [262] [PRAISE: Prefix-Based Rollout Reuse in Agentic Search Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03675)
*Erhan Zhang,Yiqun Chen,Zechun Niu,Wei Yang,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRAISE框架通过重用搜索轨迹前缀和生成中间奖励，解决智能搜索中数据效率低和奖励稀疏问题，在多项多跳问答基准上表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于搜索的强化学习方法存在两个核心限制：训练期间昂贵的长时程轨迹未充分利用，且监督通常只在最终答案处可用，导致严重的奖励稀疏问题。

Method: 提出PRAISE框架：从完整搜索轨迹中提取不同搜索轮次的前缀状态，从中获取中间答案，使用这些前缀既构建额外训练轨迹，又通过前缀间的性能差异推导步骤级奖励。使用单一共享模型进行搜索策略学习和前缀答案评估。

Result: 在多跳问答基准测试中，PRAISE始终优于强基线方法，提高了性能表现。

Conclusion: PRAISE通过重用搜索轨迹前缀和生成中间奖励，有效提高了智能搜索训练的数据效率和信用分配，无需额外人工标注或单独奖励模型。

Abstract: In agentic search, large language models (LLMs) are trained to perform multi-turn retrieval and reasoning for complex tasks such as multi-hop question answering (QA). However, current search-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods suffer from two core limitations: expensive long-horizon rollouts are under-utilized during training, and supervision is typically available only at the final answer, resulting in severe reward sparsity. We present Prefix-based Rollout reuse for Agentic search with Intermediate Step rEwards (PRAISE), a framework for improving both data efficiency and credit assignment in agentic search training. Given a complete search trajectory, PRAISE extracts prefix states at different search turns, elicits intermediate answers from them, and uses these prefixes both to construct additional training trajectories and to derive step-level rewards from performance differences across prefixes. Our method uses a single shared model for both search policy learning and prefix answer evaluation, enabling joint optimization without extra human annotations or a separate reward model. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that PRAISE consistently improves performance over strong baselines.

</details>


### [263] [Structured Multi-Criteria Evaluation of Large Language Models with Fuzzy Analytic Hierarchy Process and DualJudge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03742)
*Yulong He,Ivan Smirnov,Dmitry Fedrushkov,Sergey Kovalchuk,Ilya Revin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于层次分析法(AHP)和模糊层次分析法(FAHP)的大语言模型评估框架，并进一步开发了DualJudge混合框架，结合直觉式直接评分和结构化AHP输出，显著提升了评估的可靠性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大语言模型直接评分方法存在不一致性和不透明的问题，需要更可靠、可解释的评估框架来改进LLM评估的质量。

Method: 1) 将层次分析法(AHP)应用于LLM评估；2) 提出置信感知的模糊层次分析法(FAHP)，使用三角模糊数建模认知不确定性；3) 基于双过程理论提出DualJudge混合框架，自适应融合直接评分和结构化AHP输出。

Result: 在JudgeBench上的系统验证表明：AHP和FAHP在不同模型规模和数据集分割上都优于直接评分，FAHP在不确定比较场景中表现出更好的稳定性。DualJudge实现了最先进的性能，证明了直觉式和审慎式评估范式的互补优势。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的结构化推理为更可靠的LLM评估提供了原则性路径，结构化评估方法能够产生更校准的判断，混合框架结合了不同评估范式的优势。

Abstract: Effective evaluation of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical bottleneck, as conventional direct scoring often yields inconsistent and opaque judgments. In this work, we adapt the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP) to LLM-based evaluation and, more importantly, propose a confidence-aware Fuzzy AHP (FAHP) extension that models epistemic uncertainty via triangular fuzzy numbers modulated by LLM-generated confidence scores. Systematically validated on JudgeBench, our structured approach decomposes assessments into explicit criteria and incorporates uncertainty-aware aggregation, producing more calibrated judgments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that both crisp and fuzzy AHP consistently outperform direct scoring across model scales and dataset splits, with FAHP showing superior stability in uncertain comparison scenarios. Building on these insights, we propose \textbf{DualJudge}, a hybrid framework inspired by Dual-Process Theory that adaptively fuses holistic direct scores with structured AHP outputs via consistency-aware weighting. DualJudge achieves state-of-the-art performance, underscoring the complementary strengths of intuitive and deliberative evaluation paradigms.
